2864 — side bend

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2864 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A Pilates side bend from a seated or side-supported position that works the obliques and the sides of the trunk.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit tall on your side with your bottom hand pressed into the mat and your legs stacked.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit tall on your side with your bottom hand pressed into the mat and your legs stacked. 2864.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lift your hips so your body forms a long diagonal line. 2864.json#/instructions/1
  3. Reach your top arm overhead and bend sideways over the supporting arm. 2864.json#/instructions/2
  4. Lift back up to the long line while keeping your hips high. 2864.json#/instructions/3
  5. Lower with control and repeat, then switch sides. 2864.json#/instructions/4

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Set up in a side support with one hand on the mat and your feet stacked. 2866.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lift your hips and reach your top arm to the ceiling. 2866.json#/instructions/1

  4. Open back up and reach to the ceiling again. 2866.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your hips lifted throughout, then switch sides. 2866.json#/instructions/4

Complete ordered source instructions

  2. Do a push-up, keeping your body straight. 2961.json#/instructions/1
  3. Row one dumbbell up to your ribs without twisting your hips. 2961.json#/instructions/2

  5. Repeat the sequence with control. 2961.json#/instructions/4

Complete ordered source instructions

  2. Keep your upper body stable and your back straight. 2963.json#/instructions/1
  3. Engage your abs and lift your legs up in front of you, keeping them straight. 2963.json#/instructions/2

  5. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower your legs back down to the starting position. 2963.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 2963.json#/instructions/5
